--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: a0638893-1297-4b39-86de-4d40ddc51973
entity_slug: dreamy-sweet-treats
reviewer_name: R M
rating: 5
body: Based on recommendations, I first ordered a bday cake. I called Ms Cynthia and she was responsive and helpful. Everyone enjoyed the almond cake with Swiss merengue! It was a fabulous recommendation. A couple days later I ordered a chocolate and vanilla cake with buttercream and it was outstanding—both the vanilla and chocolate cake were moist and balanced. Loved the buttercream. Other family members ordered the chocolate pie and the pastry cream was rich and smooth.

Aside from the taste of the cakes, they were beautifully decorated. I requested particular colors for the first cake and Christmas-themed for the second without additional direction, and Ms Cynthia didn’t need it. Her designs were perfect for each cake: elegant and festive, respectively.

These are not inexpensive cakes. However, the quality is top notch.

I absolutely recommend trying Dreamy Sweet Treats and look forward to ordering again. Thanks, Ms Cynthia!
